var CQuotient = window.CQuotient = {};
CQuotient.clientId = 'bbdh-melissaanddoug';
CQuotient.realm = 'BBDH';
CQuotient.siteId = 'melissaanddoug';
CQuotient.instanceType = 'prd';
CQuotient.activities = [];
CQuotient.cqcid='';
CQuotient.cquid='';
CQuotient.initFromCookies = function () {
	var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
	for(var i=0;i < ca.length;i++) {
	  var c = ca[i];
	  while (c.charAt(0)==' ') c = c.substring(1,c.length);
	  if (c.indexOf('cqcid=') == 0) {
		  CQuotient.cqcid=c.substring('cqcid='.length,c.length);
	  } else if (c.indexOf('cquid=') == 0) {
		  CQuotient.cquid=c.substring('cquid='.length,c.length);
		  break;
	  }
	}
}
CQuotient.getCQCookieId = function () {
	if(window.CQuotient.cqcid == '')
		window.CQuotient.initFromCookies();
	return window.CQuotient.cqcid;
};
CQuotient.getCQUserId = function () {
	if(window.CQuotient.cquid == '')
		window.CQuotient.initFromCookies();
	return window.CQuotient.cquid;
};
CQuotient.trackEventsFromAC = function (/* Object or Array */ events) {
try {
	if (Object.prototype.toString.call(events) === "[object Array]") {
		events.forEach(_trackASingleCQEvent);
	} else {
		CQuotient._trackASingleCQEvent(events);
	}
} catch(err) {}
};
CQuotient._trackASingleCQEvent = function ( /* Object */ event) {
	if (event && event.id) {
		if (event.type === dw.ac.EV_PRD_DETAIL) {
			CQuotient.trackViewProduct( {id:'', alt_id: event.id, type: 'raw_sku'} );
		} // not handling the other dw.ac.* events currently
	}
};
CQuotient.trackViewProduct = function(/* Object */ cqParamData){
	var cq_params = {};
	cq_params.cookieId = CQuotient.getCQCookieId();
	cq_params.userId = CQuotient.getCQUserId();
	cq_params.product = cqParamData.product;
	cq_params.realm = cqParamData.realm;
	cq_params.siteId = cqParamData.siteId;
	cq_params.instanceType = cqParamData.instanceType;
	
	if(CQuotient.sendActivity) {
		CQuotient.sendActivity(CQuotient.clientId, 'viewProduct', cq_params);
	} else {
		CQuotient.activities.push({activityType: 'viewProduct', parameters: cq_params});
	}
};

<h3 class="hero-carousel-item-title regular-heading">Why Failure Is A Good Thing</h3>
<p class="hero-carousel-item-text light-copy">by Melissa Bernstein</p>
<a aria-label="Link Blog To: Why Failure is a good thing" class="larger-btn button red-btn" href="https://www.melissaanddoug.com/blogpost/?postId=why-failure-is-a-good-thing" tabindex="-1" title="read more">

<script type="text/javascript">

var checkJquery = setInterval(function(){
	
	if(!window.$){
		console.log('jquery undefined');
	} else{
		//jQuery detected
		clearInterval(checkJquery);

// Add desclaimer to blog posts //
var postAuthor = $('.post-single__author__name').text();
if (postAuthor.indexOf('Melissa') == -1){

$('.post-single__header__group').prepend('<p class="blog-desclaimer">PLAY NOTE: We love giving our bloggers free Melissa & Doug toys to play with in return for their honest reviews and creative ideas for fun activities. Some of our bloggers may also receive a fee for their time reviewing our products. Either way, we ask that their opinions be straight from their own imaginations!</p>');

}


// Change #takebackchildhood
if($('.navcallout-right').attr('title') == 'Sign up for Email!'){
$('.navcallout-rightspan').text('Sign up for Email!');
$('.navcallout-right').attr('href', '#pop-form')
}


		// Fix plp bg image when there is no text
		if($('.plp-container').length > 0){
			$('.grid-interruptor').each(function(){
				if($(this).find('.grid-interruptor-details').text().length < 5){

        			/* Might need later to find bg image values
        			var bg = $(this).find('.grid-interruptor-content').css('background-image');
        			bg = bg.replace('url(','').replace(')','');
        			*/
        
					$(this).find('.grid-interruptor-content').addClass('plp-full-bg');
$(this).find('.grid-interruptor-details').css('display','none');
  
				}
			});
		}

		//PZ Copy

		$('.personalizeit-trigger').on('click', function(){
			var startTime = new Date().getTime();

			var checkPZ = setInterval(function(){
				if($('.product-pz-preview #MainImage').length > 0){
		
					clearInterval(checkPZ);
					$('.product-pz-description').text('This will take an additional two days to ship and rendering is an approximation only');

				} else if(new Date().getTime() - startTime > 10000){
					clearInterval(checkPZ);
				}
			});
		});


/*
    	$.getJSON("//jsonip.com/?callback=?", function (data) {

        	if(data.ip == '75.99.252.164'){
        		$('.pdp-main--personalize-it').css('display','block');
        	} else{
        		$('.pdp-main--personalize-it').css('display','none');
        	}
    	});

*/

		//Change Category Names
		var ourToys = $('#our-toys').attr('href');
		var shopBy = $('#shop-by').attr('href');
		
		$('.main-nav--item-anchor').each(function(){
			if($(this).attr('href') == ourToys){
				$(this).html('Shop');
			} else if($(this).attr('href') == shopBy){
				$(this).html('Discover');
			}
		});
		
		//Remove age badge on all skus that match the array
		var furnitureProdSkus = ['30220','30221','30222','30223','30224','30225','30226','30227','30228','30229','30230','30231','30232','30235','30237','30238','30239','30241','30242','30244','2427','8789'];
		var isSku = $('.pdp-main--product-id');
		var skuValue = $('.pdp-main--product-id span').text();

		if(isSku.length > 0){
			if(furnitureProdSkus.indexOf(skuValue) > -1){
				$('.age-badge').css('display','none');
			}
		}

		//Remove return selector on order return
		$('.single-order__returns-section .single-order__returns-row:last-child .single-order__radio-row:last-child').css('display','none');

		//Activate change event to show PR badges
		if(window.location.href.indexOf('summer-toys-on-vacation') >-1){
			$('.pt_product-search-result .hero-grid--item').css('padding-bottom','38%');
		}
		
		// Froduct information link on plp
		var pdpContainer = $('#pdpMain');
		if(pdpContainer.length > 0){
			$('.pdp-extras--product-safety a').attr('href','http://info.MelissaAndDoug.com/information');
		}
		
		// Missing speech puble which is causing js issues
		if($('.search-results-character-callout').length > 0){
			$('.search-results-character-callout').append('<span class="speech-bubble">asdf</span>')
		}
		
		// Fixed position for nav causing issues with blog
		if(window.location.href.indexOf('blog') >-1){
			$('.post-single__header').css('cssText','position:relative !important; box-shadow:none !important;');
		}

		//Change RMA message
		$('.return__messaging-wrapper').html('To complete your return please contact us at 800-718-5365 any time Monday-Friday, 7am to 5pm EST. We ask that you have your order number on hand before calling us, so that we may assist you as quickly as possible!');

		
		// Addign INTL shipping message for Borderdree customers
		if($('.cart-header').length > 0){
			setTimeout(function(){
				if($('.header--switch-trigger img').attr('alt') == 'United States'){
					console.log('US order');
				} else{
					$('.qualified-message').remove();
					$('.order-column__cart-coupon-ruleset').append('<p class="ship-copy-desclaimer">International orders placed through Borderfree do not qualify for the $49+ promotion for free standard shipping. Free shipping is only available in the contiguous US locations only.</p>');
					$('.cart-footer').addClass('borderfree-noship');

					$('.borderfree-noship .coupon-single-row .small-column').each(function(){
						if($(this).html().indexOf('Shipping') > -1){
							$(this).addClass('bf-noship-discount');
						}
					});
				}
			},2000);

		}

	}
	
});

</script>
